Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on petrography structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 879. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 858 with relations in degree 3.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by petrography generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 879, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 858. QED.

At step n, the stability criterion invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 246. QED.
